### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* datepicker: update broken styles with baseui 13
* app: update to baseui 13
* progress-circle: remove default export
* pagination: remove default export
* notification: change Kind with KindType and PLACEMENT with PlacementType
* popover: change PLACEMENT with PopoverPlacementType
* switch: change LABEL_PLACEMENT export type for LabelPlacementType
* components: remove the button KIND and SIZE as types. Now they are const and the types are KindType & SizeType
* popover: PLACEMENT is now a const and PopoverPlacement is the type
* app: update to baseUI 12
